# MANIFEST

Generated by `python src/audit/build_manifest.py` — do not edit. Producers are extracted from the string literals in each script, so this file cannot drift from the code.

65 scripts, 1194 files under `outputs/` and `data/`.

## Layout

```
src/fetch/      pulls from an external API or dump (incremental, resumable)
src/build/      builds analysis tables and frozen samples
src/probes/     sends prompts to a model
src/analysis/   computes results, writes reports and figures
src/app/        Streamlit dashboard + pages/
src/audit/      audits the repo: data quality, prompts, this manifest
src/*.py        shared modules (prompts, metrics, baselines) + regimes/
prompts/        every prompt as a .txt template
data/           read-only inputs; only fetch scripts may write here
outputs/        everything generated
Archive/        historical, never imported or run
```

All scripts run from the repo root: `python src/<group>/<name>.py`.

## Scripts

### `src/fetch/`

_pulls from an external API or dump — incremental and resumable_

| script | what it does |
|---|---|
| `src/fetch/fetch_author_stats.py` | Fetch author-level covariates and venue data from OpenAlex for all eval_table papers |
| `src/fetch/fetch_citations_openalex.py` | Fetch OpenAlex citation counts for ICLR submissions |
| `src/fetch/fetch_citations_s2_full.py` | Full-corpus Semantic Scholar citation refetch |
| `src/fetch/fetch_citations_s2_v2.py` | Semantic Scholar citation refetch, v2 — one uniform code path for every paper |
| `src/fetch/fetch_iclr2026_citations.py` | Citation count for every ICLR 2026 accepted paper, then the distribution |
| `src/fetch/fetch_iclr2026_top_cited.py` | Find the ICLR 2026 papers that blew up — highest-cited, as of the run date |
| `src/fetch/fetch_iclr_proceedings_pdfs.py` | Download ICLR 2025 camera-ready PDFs from the official proceedings site |
| `src/fetch/fetch_openreview_pdfs.py` | Download ICLR PDFs from OpenReview, keyed by forum id |
| `src/fetch/fetch_pc_decisions.py` | Fetch Program Chair decision notes from OpenReview API for the top N outlier papers |
| `src/fetch/fetch_rejected_venues_s2.py` | Fetch where rejected ICLR papers were eventually published, via Semantic Scholar |
| `src/fetch/fetch_rejected_venues_s2_title.py` | Fetch venues for rejected ICLR papers that have NO arXiv id, via Semantic Scholar |
| `src/fetch/resolve_arxiv_fuzzy.py` | Second-pass arXiv resolution for papers exact-title matching missed, verified by abstract |
| `src/fetch/resolve_arxiv_ids.py` | Resolve every OpenReview submission to an arXiv ID, using one rule set for all papers |

### `src/build/`

_turns fetched data into analysis tables and frozen samples_

| script | what it does |
|---|---|
| `src/build/__init__.py` |  |
| `src/build/build_author_covariates.py` | Build paper-level author covariates from OpenAlex author stats |
| `src/build/build_consistent_decision_head.py` | Merge all_paper_results.csv with the GPT-OSS-20b old-RDD decision-head |
| `src/build/build_eval_table.py` | Build the single flat evaluation table used by all downstream eval scripts |
| `src/build/build_eval_table_year.py` | Build an eval table for a single submission year — used for the out-of-sample test |
| `src/build/build_leakage_ledger.py` | Flatten every leakage/recall probe run in the repo into one auditable ledger |
| `src/build/build_oos_samples.py` | Freeze the evaluation samples for the out-of-sample leakage / review benchmark |
| `src/build/build_slim_2025_papers.py` | Freeze the ICLR 2025 population the slim 9-call pipeline runs on |
| `src/build/normalize_paper_markdown.py` | Make ReviewArena text look like the text the 2018-2020 pipeline was fed |
| `src/build/rebuild_placebo.py` | Replace the placebo probe titles with real arXiv titles that were never ICLR submissions |
| `src/build/tag_fields.py` | Tag each 2018-2020 ICLR paper with a primary research field using Together AI |
| `src/build/tag_rejection_reasons.py` | Tag each outlier paper with 1-3 rejection reason labels using Claude |

### `src/probes/`

_sends prompts to a model (leakage / recall / review probes)_

| script | what it does |
|---|---|
| `src/probes/leakage_abstract_completion_v1.py` | Abstract-completion probe — verbatim text memorization test (training-data |
| `src/probes/leakage_controls.py` | Probe-validity (placebo) controls for the LAP leakage test |
| `src/probes/leakage_eval_v1.py` | Leakage evaluation v1: test whether the LLM shows anomalously good citation |
| `src/probes/leakage_fame_trace_sample.py` | Re-elicit thinking traces for a fame-stratified sample of the fame-recall probe |
| `src/probes/leakage_fame_v1.py` | Fame-recall probe — the outcome-side twin of the LAP decision probe |
| `src/probes/leakage_lap_v1.py` | LAP (Lookahead Propensity) leakage test — adapted from Gao, Jiang, Yan (2026) |
| `src/probes/leakage_masked_rereview.py` | Masked re-review — identity-ablation experiment (the mechanism test) |
| `src/probes/qualitative_check.py` | Qualitative check — run the calibrated ICLR reviewer rubric over a handful of papers |
| `src/probes/run_oos_probes.py` | Run the frozen out-of-sample probe plan against a model, keeping full reasoning traces |
| `src/probes/run_review_eval.py` | Score a year's submissions with the calibrated ICLR rubric and compare against both |
| `src/probes/run_slim_pipeline.py` | Run the slim 9-call review pipeline over ICLR 2025 accepted papers |
| `src/probes/slim_pipeline.py` | The slim 9-call conference-review pipeline, ported to the Together API |

### Files written by more than one script

These are mutated in place — one script creates the file, others add columns to it. Run order matters, and a rerun of the creator silently drops the later columns.

| file | writers |
|---|---|
| `outputs/data_audit.md` | `src/audit/build_manifest.py`, `src/audit/data_audit.py` |
| `outputs/outlier_reviews.csv` | `src/analysis/outlier_analysis.py`, `src/build/tag_rejection_reasons.py`, `src/fetch/fetch_pc_decisions.py` |

## Gaps

**0 run logs** — produced by shell redirection (`python src/... > outputs/x.log`), which no source scan can see. Owner is inferred from the filename and is a guess, not provenance.

**1070 files no script in this repo writes.** Split by whether anything reads them — the read ones are external inputs (fetched before this repo existed, or handed over), the unread ones have no traceable provenance at all and are the ones to worry about:

**23 runtime-built paths** — the producer is known but the filename depends on a flag or model name, so it cannot be matched to a file by string comparison:

- `outputs/eval_table_{year}.csv` — `src/audit/build_manifest.py, src/build/build_eval_table_year.py, src/probes/run_review_eval.py`
- `outputs/leakage_exclusion_bootstrap_{args.citation_source}{_vp}.csv` — `src/analysis/leakage_exclusion_bootstrap.py`
- `outputs/leakage_lap_report_{args.tag}.md` — `src/probes/leakage_lap_v1.py`
- `outputs/leakage_lap_traces_{args.tag}.jsonl` — `src/probes/leakage_lap_v1.py`
- `outputs/leakage_lap_{args.tag}.csv` — `src/probes/leakage_lap_v1.py`
- `outputs/leakage_masked_report{suffix}.md` — `src/probes/leakage_masked_rereview.py`
- `outputs/leakage_masked_rereview{suffix}.csv` — `src/probes/leakage_masked_rereview.py`
- `outputs/leakage_masked_traces{suffix}.jsonl` — `src/probes/leakage_masked_rereview.py`
- `outputs/oos_probes_{slug(model)}.csv` — `src/probes/run_oos_probes.py`
- `outputs/oos_traces_{slug(model)}.jsonl` — `src/probes/run_oos_probes.py`
- `outputs/oos_traces_{slug}.jsonl` — `src/build/build_leakage_ledger.py`
- `outputs/openreview_pdf_fetch_{year}.log` — `src/fetch/fetch_openreview_pdfs.py`
- `outputs/review_eval_{year}_report.md` — `src/probes/run_review_eval.py`
- `outputs/review_eval_{year}_{model_key}.csv` — `src/probes/run_review_eval.py`
- `outputs/review_eval_{year}_{model_key}_traces.jsonl` — `src/probes/run_review_eval.py`

- `outputs/samples/review_eval_{year}_sample.csv` — `src/probes/run_review_eval.py`

- `outputs/slim_2025_{tag}_traces.jsonl` — `src/probes/run_slim_pipeline.py`
